# Card audit findings

Generated by `npm run card-audit -- progress` from the scan files. Do not hand-edit.

## Rules text different (133)

- **Arcanist's Almanac** (Open Beta) `f09cc7b1-96fd-47e5-946d-324962b9efe9`
  - card: FORESIGHT -- COST – Deplete this card. -- EFFECT – Look at the top 5 cards of either deck and put them back in any order.
  - catalog: FORESIGHT -- COST: Deplete this card. -- EFFECT: Look at up to the top 5 cards of any one deck. Put them back in any order.
  - notes: Card prints 'Look at the top 5 cards of either deck'; catalog adds 'up to' ('Look at up to the top 5 cards of any one deck'). Likely newer templating, but 'up to' is a player choice of number. Card does not print 'up to' here (Nearby Serpent does).
- **Ecto-Distillation** (Open Beta) `37f34e7c-dbb2-4393-ad6a-bac2a3571ba9`
  - card: COST – Name a card. -- EFFECT – Your opponent must search your deck for all copies of the card. Put one in your hand and the rest in your discard pile.
  - catalog: EXTRA COST: Name a card. -- Search your deck for any number of copies of the card and reveal them. Put 1 into your hand and the rest into your discard pile. An opponent may search your deck for any number of copies of the card. If they do, put them into your discard pile.
  - notes: Card: your OPPONENT searches your deck for all copies; you put one in hand, rest in discard. Catalog: YOU search for any number of copies and reveal them, put 1 in hand, rest in discard, THEN an opponent may also search your deck for copies and discard them. Structure and who searches differ.

- **Beatrix Winterbottom** (Open Beta) `bb64ac1b-8361-475a-8598-0dd67ce6222b`

  - catalog: UNIQUE BEATRIX -- When you draw any number of cards, increase the amount by 1. -- While you have at least 10 cards in your hand, you cannot draw cards.

Also 'If you draw' vs catalog 'When you draw'. Likely same in practice but replacement vs prohibition could interact differently, so flagged. No artist credit in footer.

TACTICAL keyword in catalog = printed 'You may play this card whenever you could play a tactic.' Likely a deliberate later rewording; flagged strictly. No artist credit printed.

- **Ritual of the Knob & Pull** (2nd Edition) `87b8eec4-0dec-4ab0-a8da-c7cf70740890`
  - card: COST – Pick a character. X is its strength. -- EFFECT – If your opponent controls at least X face-down resources, destroy the character.
  - catalog: EXTRA COST: Pick a character. X is its strength. -- If that character's controller controls at least X face-down resources, destroy the character.
  - notes: Card checks whether YOUR OPPONENT controls at least X face-down resources; catalog checks whether 'that character's controller' does. Differs when you pick your own character (or in multiplayer). Catalog also says EXTRA COST: for COST –.
- **Irresistible Bribe** (2nd Edition) `4779dd8b-22b8-45ea-adfc-aaf6c9ce11d8`
  - card: COST – Pick a character. X is its strength. -- EFFECT – Destroy the character. Your opponent's faction gains X influence.
  - catalog: EXTRA COST: Pick a character. X is its strength. -- Destroy the character. That character’s controller's faction gains X influence.
  - notes: Card: 'Your opponent's faction gains X influence' (always the opponent, even if the picked character is yours). Catalog: 'That character's controller's faction gains X influence', which changes who gains the influence when the picked character is your own.
- **Limited Liability** (2nd Edition) `5f4ac8a0-c748-40dd-b591-1afabeb306fa`
  - card: This turn, cards can only be destroyed by costs, and all inflicted damage is reduced to 0.
  - catalog: This turn, cards in play and cards deployed can only be destroyed by costs, and all inflicted damage is reduced to 0.
  - notes: Card: 'cards can only be destroyed by costs'. Catalog adds a scope clause: 'cards in play and cards deployed can only be destroyed by costs'. Probably a clarification, but it restricts which cards are covered, so flagged for triage.
- **Hidden Sandwich** (2nd Edition) `0254473b-3a7a-418f-b541-74a3a57d2978`
  - card: ATTACH TO CHARACTER -- You may play this card whenever you could play a tactic. -- If the attached character is destroyed, destroy this card instead. Reduce the amount of damage inflicted to the character this turn to 0.
  - catalog: TACTICAL -- ATTACH TO CHARACTER -- If the attached character would be destroyed, destroy this card instead. Reduce the amount of damage already inflicted to the character this turn to 0.
  - notes: Probably templating only, flagged for verification. Catalog has TACTICAL keyword where card prints 'You may play this card whenever you could play a tactic'; catalog says 'would be destroyed' where card says 'is destroyed'; catalog adds 'already' in 'damage already inflicted to the character this turn' (card omits it).
- **Cheats McGuillicutty** (2nd Edition) `078b8b9a-b559-44b9-b86d-7cee8727b3ad`
  - card: UNIQUE PLUCK -- The top card of your deck is revealed at all times. -- BOTTOM DEAL -- COST – Pay 1. -- EFFECT – Put the top card of your deck on the bottom. -- CHEAT -- COST – Deplete this card. -- EFFECT – Reveal the top card of your deck. You may play it. (You can't play resources with this ability.) -- CHANGE COSTUME -- COST – Pay 3. Shuffle this card into its owner's deck. -- EFFECT – Search your deck for a "pluck" card with a different name than this card and put it into play.
  - catalog: UNIQUE PLUCK -- The top card of your deck is revealed at all times. -- BOTTOM DEAL -- COST: Pay 1. -- EFFECT: Put the top card of your deck on the bottom. -- CHEAT -- COST: Deplete this card. -- EFFECT: Reveal the top card of your deck. You may deploy it. (You can't play resources with this ability.) -- CHANGE COSTUME -- COST: Pay 3. Shuffle this card into its owner's deck. -- EFFECT: Search your deck for a pluck card with a different name than this card and reveal it. Put it into play under your control.
  - notes: CHANGE COSTUME: card prints 'Search your deck for a "pluck" card with a different name than this card and put it into play.' Catalog adds 'and reveal it' (a reveal that is not printed) and 'under your control'. Revealing the searched card is an information change, so flagged different. (CHEAT 'deploy' vs printed 'play' is just templating.) Confirmed on a zoomed crop.
- **Counter Attack** (2nd Edition) `17565f9a-fdd4-488c-889f-03fa430f358b`
  - card: COST – Play this only during your opponent's turn and not during a battle. -- EFFECT – Attack your opponent. Draw a card. (This effect only allows you to attack once. You must resolve the battle before you draw the card.)
  - catalog: EXTRA COST: Deploy this only during an opponent's turn and not during a battle. -- Attack that player. Draw a card. (This effect only allows you to attack once. You must resolve the battle immediately and before you draw the card.)
  - notes: Reminder text prints 'You must resolve the battle before you draw the card.' Catalog says 'You must resolve the battle immediately and before you draw the card.' (extra word 'immediately', a timing requirement not printed). Flagged different out of caution. Other differences are templating: EXTRA COST / 'Deploy' for printed COST / 'Play'; 'Attack that player' for printed 'Attack your opponent'; 'an opponent's turn'.
- **Scrag** (2nd Edition) `a41131b1-d9ab-404b-bd55-1fa9d9c77dcf`
  - card: COST – Pick a character. X is its life. -- EFFECT – If your opponent has at least X cards in their discard pile, destroy the character.

  - notes: Card checks 'your opponent' has at least X cards in their discard pile; catalog checks 'that character's controller' (changes whose discard pile is counted when targeting your own character). Also printed 'COST –' vs catalog 'EXTRA COST:'.
- **Two-Headed Coin** (2nd Edition) `90a28359-1bad-419f-bd94-1566b810853f`
  - card: If you lose a coin flip, you may shuffle this card into your deck. If you do, reflip that coin and draw a card instead.
  - catalog: If you would lose a coin flip, you may shuffle this card into its owner's deck. If you do, reflip that coin and use that result instead and draw a card.
  - notes: Card says shuffle into 'your deck' (catalog: 'its owner's deck', differs for a card you control but don't own) and 'reflip that coin and draw a card instead' (catalog adds 'would lose' and 'use that result instead'; printed text does not say the new result is used). Footer reads 'Limited edition artwork by FangWei'; 'Artist Series' watermark.
- **Unexpected Boobysnake** (2nd Edition) `0da66705-4bbd-49c7-981a-3bf32eb437b7`
  - card: You may play this card whenever you could play a tactic. If you do, when this card enters play discard a card or destroy this card.
  - catalog: TACTICAL -- When this card enters play, if you deployed it during an opponent's turn, discard a card or destroy this card.
  - notes: Printed: discard-or-destroy applies whenever you played it using the tactic-timing permission ('If you do'). Catalog restricts it to 'if you deployed it during an opponent's turn', which differs when it is played at tactic timing during your own turn.
- **Blow Up** (2nd Edition) `be0aa55a-b127-4a2c-af8e-dca2724e15b6`

- **Needless Beating** (2nd Edition) `06b104b7-eb32-4a44-9b33-587c0345715d`
  - card: COST – Pick a character. -- EFFECT – Inflict 2 damage to the character. If your opponent's faction has 15 influence or less, inflict 1 additional damage to the character. If your opponent's faction has 10 influence or less, inflict 1 damage to each character your opponent controls. If your opponent's faction has 5 influence or less, draw a card.

If that player's faction has 15 influence or less, inflict 1 additional damage to the character. If that player's faction has 10 influence or less, inflict 1 damage to each character they control. If that player's faction has 5 influence or less, draw a card.

Pick a character that player controls.' which restricts the target to that opponent's characters, and catalog refers to 'that player's faction' instead of 'your opponent's faction'. Same in a 2-player game except the printed card can target any character. Multiplayer re-templating but a target restriction change, so marked different.

Multiplayer re-templating, but could change gameplay, so marked different.
